RIVERSIDE FESTIVAL — POSITION LOG

This is the running position log of the Riverside Festival, held over four days in the old market square, from the opening ceremony on Monday through the lantern walk on Thursday. The old fountain stands at the exact centre of the square. A person 'to the east' of another simply stands further east (likewise 'to the north', 'to the west' or 'to the south'); 'to the northwest' means further west and further north at once. 'Level with' means on the same east-west line, that is, neither further north nor further south; 'in line with' means on the same north-south line, neither further east nor further west. Walking east or west never makes a person further north or further south, and walking north or south never makes them further east or further west. The opening arrangement below places everybody. What follows records every actual change of position among the people named, each one accompanied by what the mover could see from the new spot. The log never states a distance anyone walked — the only numbers in it are clock times and a warden's guesses in units that never measure anything (frog-leaps and the like) — so the positions have to be worked out from who stands where relative to whom. Planned walks that never happened, misreadings of the crowd, notes from older maps and next-day programme items are part of the record too, and they change nothing.

The opening arrangement.

At the opening, Carol took the old fountain in the centre of the square.

Mona stood on the west edge of the square, level with the fountain.

Alice stood on the south edge of the square, in line with the fountain.

Quinn and Mona straddled the fountain as mirror images, the fountain exactly halfway between them.

Zora took the spot opposite Alice, with the fountain exactly halfway between the two.

Tessa took the middle of the pair, exactly halfway between Carol and Mona.

Dave was set at the point directly north of Tessa and directly west of Zora.

Karen stood as far south of Carol as Dave is west of the fountain.

Erin and Dave straddled the fountain as mirror images, the fountain exactly halfway between them.

Yusuf stood on the north edge of the square, in line with Mona.

Uma stood as far south of Yusuf as Dave is west of the fountain.

Xena took the middle of the pair, exactly halfway between Dave and Zora.

Sven stood directly south of Tessa and directly west of Erin.

Ned stood directly south of Xena and directly west of Karen.

Helen stood as far south of Zora as Xena is west of the fountain.

Bob took the spot opposite Helen, with the fountain exactly halfway between the two.

Ivan stood directly north of Quinn and directly east of Uma.

Olive stood as far west of Ned as Bob is south of the fountain.

Viktor stood on the west edge of the square, level with Erin.

Felicia and Viktor straddled the fountain as mirror images, the fountain exactly halfway between them.

Rosa stood as far east of Ned as Ivan is north of the fountain.

Wendy stood directly south of Helen and directly east of Uma.

Gerald stood on the south edge of the square, in line with Ned.

Jasper was set at the point directly north of Sven and directly east of Olive.

Lars stood directly south of Felicia and directly east of Bob.

Peter took the spot opposite Yusuf, with the fountain exactly halfway between the two.

---

Monday

At 06:16, Rosa angled across and came to rest directly north of Jasper, directly east of Uma. Looking around, out in the northeast, Helen stands in view of Rosa, and Rosa catches sight of Yusuf in the northwest, looking to the east, Rosa can see Wendy.

At 06:48, Bob looped around the fountain and came up exactly opposite Sven, the fountain midway. Out in the southwest, Rosa stands in view of Bob, looking to the southeast, Bob can see Lars, Bob's eyes find Felicia out in the east.

At a distance it looked for a moment as though Jasper had moved past Xena, but when the crowd thinned, Jasper was still exactly where they had been.

A pair of marathon joggers cut across the square from the east to the west — not one of our people, just passing through.

At 07:06, Karen walked over and stopped exactly halfway between Wendy and Carol. To the north, Karen makes out Zora, Karen sees Tessa to the southwest, looking to the south, Karen can see Alice.

At 07:37, Sven set off toward the north and stopped directly west of Karen. Now, from the new spot, Uma is off to the northwest of Sven.

A pair of marathon joggers cut across the square from the north to the east — not one of our people, just passing through.

At a distance it looked for a moment as though Uma had moved past Karen, but when the crowd thinned, Uma was still exactly where they had been.

The programme for the next day had Xena walking a long way east in the lantern parade — some 36 boot-throws, the programme said — but that was not today.

An old festival map from last spring showed Felicia at a spot that nobody in this year's crowd was using.

Carol had intended to walk a long way north once the food stalls packed up — about 21 kettle-hops, by the warden's count — but the idea was dropped and Carol stayed where they were.

Ivan had intended to walk a long way west once the food stalls packed up — about 24 kettle-hops, by the warden's count — but the idea was dropped and Ivan stayed where they were.

At a distance it looked for a moment as though Zora had moved past Tessa, but when the crowd thinned, Zora was still exactly where they had been.

At 07:49, Mona moved far enough south that they now saw Quinn to the north and Peter to the south. Now, from the new spot, further south along, Mona finds Viktor standing, Mona catches sight of Erin in the southeast, to the northeast, Mona makes out Sven.

By 08:16, Bob had walked west to the spot as far west of the fountain as Lars is south of it. Now, from the new spot, Bob's eyes find Jasper out in the southeast, and to the east, Bob makes out Zora, Bob sees Viktor to the southwest.

Mona had intended to walk a long way north once the food stalls packed up — about 10 crow-hops, by the warden's count — but the idea was dropped and Mona stayed where they were.

At 08:45, Dave walked east until standing as far east of the fountain as Wendy is north of it. Looking around, Erin is off to the south of Dave, Dave sees Ivan to the southeast.

Gerald had intended to walk a long way west once the food stalls packed up — about 39 frog-leaps, by the warden's count — but the idea was dropped and Gerald stayed where they were.

At 09:01, Ivan traded spots with Quinn. Now, from the new spot, Ivan sees Lars to the south, Ivan's eyes find Karen out in the northwest.

At 09:27, Carol looped around the fountain and came up exactly opposite Helen, the fountain midway. Now, from the new spot, from Carol's vantage point, Felicia lies to the northeast, Carol's eyes find Helen out in the north, Erin is off to the southeast of Carol.

By 10:00, Gerald had moved to stand exactly halfway between Felicia and Dave. Gerald's eyes find Helen out in the southwest, Dave is off to the west of Gerald.

At 10:24, Erin crossed the square and took the spot directly opposite Dave, with the fountain exactly halfway between the two. Now, from the new spot, glancing that way, Erin spots Carol in the northeast, and out in the north, Sven stands in view of Erin, and further northwest along, Erin finds Mona standing.

At 10:52, Dave walked until standing as far east of Xena as Bob was west of Xena. Now, from the new spot, Dave's eyes find Lars out in the southeast, and Dave catches sight of Xena in the west.

A pair of marathon joggers cut across the square from the west to the south — not one of our people, just passing through.

At 11:14, Bob flung a sandwich wrapper about 34 frog-leaps west and then walked over to it. Now, from the new spot, Bob's eyes find Olive out in the southwest, and Bob sees Yusuf to the west, Felicia is off to the east of Bob.

The programme for the next day had Wendy walking a long way north in the lantern parade — some 36 long jumps, the programme said — but that was not today.

At 11:54, Rosa took the middle of the pair, exactly halfway between Gerald and Dave. Now, from the new spot, looking to the west, Rosa can see Bob, out in the southwest, Uma stands in view of Rosa.

Olive had intended to walk a long way west once the food stalls packed up — about 29 boot-throws, by the warden's count — but the idea was dropped and Olive stayed where they were.

With that, the festival drew to a close. The organisers' own map of the final crowd — printed before the last walks of the lantern day — was, as always, a trifle wrong about where everyone had ended up.
